# Stage 2 findings: focused answer/content pathways

**Within this pilot, candidate-family perturbations reach a layer17 key-input route and a smaller head-specific query route. The direct paths are modest relative to ordinary family effects. Joint family patches can reduce the candidate-only correction, consistent with intervention-induced occlusion/interference. Neither result identifies a pressure-specific circuit or strategic intent.**

This synthesis follows the pre-result [interpretation rubric](stage2-analysis-plan.md). It uses only MAIN `stage2-main-6a2ea52-20261021T005608Z`, never the376calibration rows. Operator confirmed job23068441 COMPLETED0:0;51,136/51,136rows, zero unmeasured,7,360forwards,1,088parent-baseline checksum checks passed. Controls pass. Independently recomputed interaction DIDs exactly equal joint−candidate−other+baseline (max discrepancy0).

## Prespecified diagnostic: H/N→P with changed donor truth and the same false emitted item

This stratum distinguishes effects from merely copying donor emission, while remaining content-confounded. Discovery35rows/24stories; confirmation41rows/24stories. The latter contains H21rows/21stories and N20rows/20stories. Estimate each story equally after averaging its eligible H/N rows. Below, links are ordered by absolute **discovery** effect, not confirmation selection; intervals are exploratory, unadjusted pointwise95%story CIs,2,000bootstrap replicates. All39fixed paths, H/N separately, both scopes and both splits are retained in the inventory below.

| Candidate→receiver, common sender scope | Discovery | Confirmation |
|---|---|---|
| L13H20→L17Kgroup6 | +.172 [.132,.209] | +.201 [.157,.243] |
| L13H21→L17Kgroup6 | +.150 [.117,.184] | +.181 [.141,.219] |
| L12H6→L17Kgroup6 | +.132 [.096,.168] | +.147 [.110,.186] |
| L13H5→L17H24Q | +.074 [.045,.105] | +.096 [.073,.121] |
| L13H5→L17Kgroup6 | +.060 [.035,.087] | +.054 [.032,.077] |
| L14H24→L17H24Q | +.046 [.017,.075] | +.085 [.059,.110] |

Kgroup6 changes the keys read by query heads24–27, **not uniquely L17H24**. Q24 is head-specific. K/Q/V patches differ in intervention size and cannot be treated as equally sized mechanistic units. The directional pattern is compatible with a contribution through attention-selection inputs under this intervention; it does not prove an abstract selector or pressure-reader role.

The leading K effects persist separately in confirmation H and N donors: L13H20 +.184 [.139,.229]/+.243 [.181,.297]; L13H21 +.154 [.121,.189]/+.212 [.156,.263]; L12H6 +.149 [.106,.193]/+.174 [.127,.220]. L13H5→Q24 gives +.079 [.051,.109]/+.129 [.097,.165]. These donors emit the recipient's same false item, so the positive truth-margin changes are not explained by imposing that emitted item alone.

For those three K paths, readout-only sender injection is near zero in confirmation (−.007,−.003,+.001 versus common+.201,+.181,+.147). In contrast L13H5→Q24 survives readout-only injection (+.098 [.070,.125]). This localizes a scope dependence of the *tested perturbation*, not a general all-token mechanism.

A smaller V route exists: L13H5→L17Vgroup6 discovery+.018 [.003,.032], confirmation+.019 [.005,.032]; confirmation H+.015 [.001,.030], N+.031 [.010,.053]. It is not appropriate to describe V as absent. Direct readout effects are small: L17H26→READOUT discovery+.011 [.003,.019], confirmation+.015 [.007,.023], with H+.005 [−.007,.015], N+.022 [.011,.033]. The fixed-grid appendix contains the remaining weak, null and reversed cells. Do not sum path effects or compute fractions of family effects; the frozen-edge and ordinary-family estimands differ and paths interact.

## Reciprocal directions and honest damage

Leading direct K links are not symmetric honesty toggles. In unchanged-truth confirmation P→H, L13H20/L13H21/L12H6→Kgroup6 effects are −.010 [−.020,−.000],−.012 [−.020,−.004],−.012 [−.023,−.001]. P→N gives −.003 [−.019,.013],−.010 [−.021,.002],+.011 [.001,.021]. L13H5→Q24 gives P→H−.021 [−.039,.000], P→N−.039 [−.071,−.008]. Thus the positive primary changed-truth H/N→P paths do not establish a donor-independent pressure signal or explain the large reverse family harm by themselves.

Ordinary union patches leave other components free and show much larger harm. On same-truth common-scope P→H/P→N across48stories, candidate6 loses10/37 and5/39baseline-accessible truths; selector2 loses16/37 and13/39; candidate+selector29/37 and28/39; candidate+outsider30/37 and25/39. These are forced first-token item outcomes, not external benchmark deception rates or free-generation quality.

Half-dose shared32 loses12/37H and7/39N truths, versus Stage1full-dose36/37 and35/39. Its P←H/P←N margin gains are+1.33/+1.34, compared with full-dose+2.49/+2.74. Reducing dose reduces both transfer and collateral under these donors. This is one partial dose and cannot establish a full efficacy–utility frontier, linearity or redundancy. Stage1/Stage2intervention outputs need not be bit-identical despite exact recaptured baseline provenance; tiny numerical differences are visible in some ordinary singles and near-threshold argmax counts.

## Family interactions: conditional interference under replacement

In the prespecified primary common-scope stratum:

| Quantity | Discovery35rows/24stories | Confirmation41rows/24stories |
|---|---|---|
| Candidate6 alone | +1.670 [1.045,2.273] | +1.762 [1.364,2.179] |
| Selector2 alone | +.035 [−.088,.160] | +.061 [−.090,.218] |
| Candidate+selector | +1.103 [.632,1.553] | +1.082 [.745,1.454] |
| Interaction C,S | −.602 [−.818,−.372] | −.740 [−.909,−.573] |
| Outsider2 alone | +.678 [.383,.959] | +.848 [.607,1.081] |
| Candidate+outsider | +1.331 [.700,1.943] | +1.489 [1.072,1.933] |
| Interaction C,O | −1.017 [−1.315,−.712] | −1.120 [−1.323,−.926] |

The negative DIDs are not reciprocal pooling artifacts. Confirmation H-only/N-only C,S DIDs are−.637 [−.870,−.393]/−.984 [−1.309,−.646]; C,O−.941 [−1.212,−.675]/−1.488 [−1.890,−1.080]. For matched unchanged truth the sign follows the direction of the singles: C,S P←H/P←N DIDs−.608/−.785, H←P/N←P+.454/+.750; this describes subadditive transfer in either direction, not an intrinsically beneficial or harmful interaction sign.

In the changed-truth primary stratum, adding selector replacement reduces correction below candidate-only, despite selector alone being near zero. **One intervention-level explanation is that replacing selector outputs with a fixed donor state overwrites the downstream state that would otherwise respond to candidate injection.** Outsider replacement may similarly alter converging routes. This is consistent with conditional dependence/occlusion and compatible with the measured candidate→Q/K links. It does not prove a natural competitive circuit, serial mediation, a backup circuit or redundancy; unions intrinsically can block signals that freely evolving recipient components would carry. Nonlinear margin compression and unmeasured routes remain alternatives.

## Limits and accounting

Direct paths freeze every other component in pass1 and let downstream components evolve in pass2. Ordinary family interactions apply no such freeze. No-sender/self controls validate this execution, not semantic identification. MLPs and untested attention routes lie outside the measured path graph. Null direct paths cannot exclude multi-hop, MLP, family-interaction or unmapped-position effects. No optional position followups were run; all39fixed paths are retained. Confirmation is within the same historically filtered SDR distribution used to discover masks, not independent OOD validation. Pointwise CIs are exploratory across39paths and many strata; no multiplicity-controlled semantic-role claim is warranted.

## Interpretation guardrails
Use stage2-analysis-plan.md. Q/K/V are not equally sized interventions (KV groups affect multiple query heads). Path effects cannot be summed into mediated fractions. Null direct edges leave multi-hop/MLP/omitted-position routes open. Same-layer exclusions are not nulls. Changed-truth donors confound content. Pure pressure, semantic role, intent, completeness and external efficacy are not identified.
